Stopping rule requested before this batch: stop at the first run in which ECP is
strictly faster than all three Beelen-type decoders in at least 48 of 50 combinations.
All attempts in this batch are retained below, including any that fail this threshold.
This outcome-dependent stopping rule selects a favorable timing run and does not
estimate how often ECP wins across independent runs. It is not a replacement for
the original paper data. The preceding standalone diagnostic run had 47/50 wins.
The decoder program, seed, sampling, warmup, order and timing boundaries are unchanged.
A local wrapper records the existing means after measurement and counts strict wins
using unrounded means; displayed timings remain rounded to four decimal places.
Each attempt starts a fresh SageMath process.
